Generative civil and mechanical design team

Executed provisional design development. 192 detailed variations across 6 contexts; 5 contexts have passing known checks. All 63 packages remain open for integrated engineering and manufacturing acceptance. An unsolved design is retained as unsolved.

Team and iterationCivil/mechanical propagationDesign variationsAll components and packagesEarlier campaign review

Team and iteration

Configuration coordinator → civil and mechanical designers → manufacturing integrator → separate critic → changed-variable proposal. The reproducible runner executes engineering adapters built by the collaborating agents. It does not simulate new LLM conversations or appoint a design authority.

Each attempt retains variables, units, materials, connection assumptions, load context, manufacturing route, check results and parent/repair reasons. A failed check proposes bounded changes. Repair mode stops at the first local numerical pass. Explicit optimization mode continues over sourced mass and quantity objectives, retaining a conditional Pareto set; every qualification gate remains open. Identical inputs are not repeated to manufacture evidence. A fresh source-bound run is required after a changed model or accepted basis.

Repeated transfer entries distinguish 20 m and 25 m spans. Proportional axle scaling is an explicit sensitivity, not an approved axle/CG allocation. LM3 and six-car planning inputs remain separate. Civil width changes recalculate section properties, self-weight and prestress responses before cage, pad and footing screening.

Detailed variations and retained failures

mechanical · LM3

optimization-budget-exhausted. 32 retained evaluations; 32 newly computed. Diagnostic/shortlist: mechanical-detail-3327b3d533ff57.

Search mode: optimization. Conditional Pareto alternatives: none (repair mode or objectives unresolved).

Critic disposition: reject-known-failures. This is an automated contract challenge; qualified independent engineering review remains required.

Best retained calculation and unresolved checks
  • pass global_service_deflection: 0.009054273029759589 m; limit 0.0123. Full body UDL and overhang equilibrium; three EI-proportional members under rigid-tie hypothesis. L/1000 is an unaccepted research target.
  • pass gross_member_normal_stress: 55322794.10155929 Pa; limit 235000000. Elastic stress under explicit 1.25 vertical multiplier, not rail-standard load combinations or accepted design resistance.
  • pass gross_web_shear: 7954559.95488146 Pa; limit 135677313.25956208. Average web shear; stress concentration, openings and shear buckling interaction not represented.
  • pass side_flange_elastic_plate_buckling: 55322794.10155929 Pa; limit 7224833963.062622. Ideal isolated outstand k=0.43, elastic plate formula; diagnostic only, no railway member buckling qualification.
  • pass side_web_elastic_plate_buckling: 55322794.10155929 Pa; limit 377755659.2178929. Ideal internal plate under uniform compression k=4 used conservatively versus maximum bending stress; actual stress-gradient/shear interaction unknown.
  • pass centre_plate_elastic_buckling: 28030215.67812337 Pa; limit 4149724182.0003896. Ideal simply-supported internal plate k=4; no postbuckling, imperfections or fabricated corner resistance adopted.
  • pass bolster_bending: 36982774.74214544 Pa; limit 235000000. Transverse half-bolster cantilever carrying EI-proportional side-member reaction into central support pad.
  • pass bolster_tip_deflection: 0.0003020259937275211 m; limit 0.001. Local cantilever displacement diagnostic; finite transverse stiffness still requires shell/beam model of actual joint compatibility.
  • pass support_diaphragm_nominal_compression: 30363926.062159058 Pa; limit 235000000. Two stiffener planes per intersection, full-width plate area excluding centre web; local crippling and diaphragm weld qualification remain unknown.
  • pass intersection_weld_nominal_shear: 82994731.23656808 Pa; limit 120000000. Two effective weld runs across each longitudinal-member width carry vertical reaction; no fatigue or eccentricity resistance assumed.
  • pass longitudinal_flange_weld_shear_flow: 11073872.422012456 Pa; limit 120000000. VQ/I divided between two longitudinal flange-web fillet throats, with same vertical multiplier.
  • pass weld_size_thinner_plate: 0.004 m; limit 0.0084. Research fabrication throat/thinner-plate limit; increasing weld without plate thickness can introduce a new failed check.
  • pass support_bolt_combined_nominal_stress: 342051840.5899998 Pa; limit 400000000. Two-row bolt group, explicit 0.3g horizontal hypothesis and 1m moment arm. sqrt(sigma^2+3*tau^2), not a connection-code interaction or crash assessment; pad bears vertical load.
  • pass support_pad_bearing: 2769997.33964247 Pa; limit 235000000. Vertical reaction divided by net pad footprint; bogie mating body, contact pressure distribution and prying not qualified.
  • pass bolt_hole_edge_distance: 0.033 m; limit 0.06. Research edge >= 1.5 hole diameters; actual bolt standard and fatigue layout require acceptance.
  • pass socket_neighbour_access: 0.051 m; limit 0.06666666666666667. 1.7d+12mm assumed socket outside diameter plus 5mm gap; actual wrench sweep and service access not proven.
  • pass crossbearer_floor_tributary_stress: 62955881.24953071 Pa; limit 235000000. Full body load treated as uniform floor demand on largest actual bearer spacing; each side half-span simply supported.
  • pass crossbearer_floor_tributary_deflection: 0.00048200596581671955 m; limit 0.001. Local floor bearer deflection under largest actual tributary pitch; shell participation not claimed.
  • fail source_side_depth_envelope: 0.75 m; limit 0.26. Existing LM3 260 mm side-sill / 220x380 mm centre-spine review envelopes, not certified fabricated sections.
  • pass xp4000_plate_length: 3.2 m; limit 4.0. Every proposed plate must fit published XP4000 cutting envelope; long stock cannot be processed as if a 4m machine were a 16m machine.
  • pass xp4000_plate_width: 0.7 m; limit 2.0. Rotation allowed on the flat cutting bed, not an assumed larger cutting table.
  • pass xp4000_plate_thickness: 0.025 m; limit 0.032. Specific XP brochure states 32mm maximum; power source, grade, piercing and finish capability still need vendor confirmation.
  • pass xp4000_individual_plate_load: 281.344 kg; limit 2010. Largest individual plate, not a whole loaded nesting sheet. A complete nest load and extraction trial remain unaccepted.
  • pass proposed_fixture_length: 16.5 m; limit 20. 20m assembly fixture reservation only; origin, capacity, stiffness and anchorage unknown.
  • pass proposed_fixture_payload: 9642.017576574392 kg; limit 25000. 25t placeholder assembly fixture payload is not offered rated equipment; total suspended load/lifting plan unaccepted.
  • unknown end_load_transfer_and_stiffness_geometry: None not evaluated; limit None. Constant EI extends over the full 16.5/18.5m body, whereas native side and centre members terminate 0.25m and 0.35m before each body end. End framing, load introduction and piecewise stiffness are absent. The beam is a declared analytical abstraction, not verified exact native geometry performance.
  • unknown bolster_underfloor_gauge_and_source_compatibility: None not evaluated; limit None. Proposed bolster is below the longitudinal members. Its vertical placement, suspension travel, floor height and swept gauge are not reconciled with the complete source assembly; source side-depth alone does not demonstrate source compatibility.
  • unknown accepted_materials_and_rail_loads: None not evaluated; limit None. Certified grade/subgrade/through-thickness, minimum properties by thickness and temperature, AW3/crash/track-twist/braking/fatigue load combinations and authority acceptance missing.
  • unknown lateral_torsional_and_connection_stability: None not evaluated; limit None. Lateral-torsional buckling, local crippling, connection eccentricity/prying, floor discontinuities, elastic load sharing and full 3D nonlinear model unverified.
  • unknown qualified_plate_splices_and_welds: None not evaluated; limit None. All long-plate segment boundaries require detailed groove, stagger/avoidance of stress hotspots, fatigue class, WPS/PQR, distortion and NDT acceptance; no butt weld is treated as qualified by geometry.
  • unknown bogie_mating_interface_and_bolt_qualification: None not evaluated; limit None. Bogie mating pad/bolt supplier drawing, bolt grade/pretension/slip/fatigue/bearing/net section, pad bending, prying and rotation remain unknown.
  • unknown machine_and_stock_order_configuration: None not evaluated; limit None. Dalzell lead does not establish live plate supply or UK melt origin. Offered XP4000 power source, full nest load, plate flatness, supplier samples and tolerance capability absent.
  • unknown complete_assembled_mass_and_fastener_origin: None not evaluated; limit None. Net plate/RHS geometry mass includes finished holes, but bolt/nut/washer, deposited weld metal, coating and remaining vehicle masses are not modeled. Full as-built mass/CG, certified property class, M27 availability where proposed, offered fastener geometry and British manufacturing origin remain unconfirmed.
  • unknown vehicle_system_and_mass_closure: None not evaluated; limit None. All gross proposed steel was added to the original car load. Replacement delta, certified axle ratings, CG, crashworthiness, route gauge, doors, floor, traction, brakes, electrical/fire systems and first article remain open.
